# HeroAI Watchdog Session Log

This file is shared between you (Jonas) and Claude. Both can read and append.

## How to use

- **Claude reads this file** at the start of each session and after each major change
- **You can append notes manually** at any time — e.g., "just clicked Resign button", "started Asterius farm", "saw lag spike on Healderv"
- **Watchdogs append automatically** — crashes, deaths, wipes, resigns, errors, map changes, strategy changes
- Sections are timestamped. Older entries stay; this is an append-only journal

## Format conventions

```
## Session started YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS
- `epoch_ms` acct=`name` **EVENT** `data`     ← from log_tailer
- **CRASH** `When` account=`name` `source` — Assertion text     ← from crash_watcher
    > last error line 1
    > last error line 2
- **NOTE** (manual) <your message>     ← optional, you write these
```

## Manual notes you can use as templates

```
- **NOTE** Started session, all 6 clients launched cleanly
- **NOTE** Asterius farm started, hard mode
- **NOTE** Clicked HeroAI Resign button on master
- **NOTE** Crashed Healderv at ~17:30 during messaging interact
```

## Phase 0 fix verification checklist (next session)

After Phase 0 (Player.py map-readiness guard) is live, watch for:

- [ ] Zero new `AvSelect.cpp:780` crashes in this file
- [ ] At least one `Player.Interact GUARD_SKIP_MAP_NOT_READY` console log on a map transition (proves the guard activated)
- [ ] HeroAI Messaging buttons (Resign / Pixelstack / Interact / Get Dialog / PCons) still work
- [ ] InventoryPlus still identifies + salvages + deposits on outpost return

---

## End of session 2026-04-06T22:53:33

### Investigation: AutoSalvage timeouts (deferred to next restart)
**Source:** `Py4GWCoreLib/routines_src/yield_src/items.py:85`
**Constant:** `queue_wait_timeout_ms = 5000` (5 seconds, hardcoded)
**Mechanism:** `Items.SalvageItems()` enqueues each item via `ActionQueueManager().AddAction("SALVAGE", ...)` then calls `yield from _wait_for_empty_queue("SALVAGE", timeout_ms=5000)`. Under CPU pressure on 8 simultaneous clients, the SALVAGE queue doesn't drain in 5s reliably -- 37 timeout warnings this session (down from 75 pre-heartbeat-fix, so improvement is real but not eliminated).
**Proposed fix:** Bump `queue_wait_timeout_ms = 5000 -> 15000` (3x). Same one-line config approach as the SHMEM_SUBSCRIBE_TIMEOUT 500->2000 fix that recovered Necro Reid + Freydis. Single point of change, low risk, addresses CPU-starvation symptom.
**Status:** Not applied this session per `feedback_no_bundled_fixes`. Apply on next restart cycle as a standalone fix, then verify warning count drops.

### Today's wins (confirmed)
- **Heartbeat fix** (`Globals.py` SHMEM_SUBSCRIBE_TIMEOUT 500->2000): Necro Reid 2x cast rate, Freydis 2.4x. Independent confirmation via shm_history is_casting% (1.3% -> 4.1% for Necro Reid).
- **ConsoleLogger widget**: 5273 console lines captured, GUARD_SKIP signal pipeline ready (just needs Phase 0 to actually fire one).
- **SharedMemoryDumper widget**: 2200+ snapshots captured, per-account state visible.
- **Screenshot pipeline rewrite**: AMSI block discovered + bypassed via Python ctypes + PrintWindow + PIL. Verified end-to-end with multimodal Read tool (first time).
- **Per-client tracking**: 8 install dirs correctly mapped via QueryFullProcessImageName P/Invoke.
- **summarize.py**: one-command session retrospective.
- **verify_watchdog.py**: NEW health check (would have caught the silent screenshot failure).
- **Log rotation**: NEW helper in `_watchdog_lib.py`, wired into log_tailer for events.jsonl (50MB cap, 3 backups).
- **Launcher hook**: Rewrote to spawn each watcher directly via `subprocess.Popen + CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE` instead of the broken `cmd /c start /MIN bat` chain.
- **Zero new crashes** this session (vs 2 AvSelect crashes earlier in the day).

### Three things to verify in tomorrow's first session
1. **Phase 0 GUARD_SKIP_MAP_NOT_READY signal** -- has been 0 fires so far (ambiguous: working silently or never loaded). Run `summarize.py` after first farm run, look for "GUARD_FIRED" lines.
2. **Screenshot watcher on REAL GW windows** -- only tested on a non-GW foreground window so far. Run `verify_watchdog.py` ~10 min after launching GW; PENDING should change to PASS once first 5-min cycle captures all 8 GW windows.
3. **Launcher auto-spawn** -- the rewritten `_spawn_watchdog()` should fire automatically when launcher starts. Verify by checking `clients/` dir mtime within ~60s of launcher start (no manual python spawn needed).

### Deferred (for future sessions)
- **AutoSalvage timeout fix** (`items.py:85` 5000->15000) -- ready, one-line, do as standalone restart cycle
- **Phase 2-5: fold InventoryPlus/Xunlai/Travel into HeroAI as wrappers** -- big undertaking, fresh session needed
- **Sarahtonin gear**: blocked by NCSoft 24h trade hold; once expired, equip dagger and verify cast rate climbs to ~30/min
- **Install dir mapping**: 5 of 8 install dirs still TBD which character. Ask Jonas next session.
- **Noise warnings**: HeroAI ensure_initialized + Skills Unlocker info-as-warning -- log level cleanup
